TreeMap: third-party notices for the native scan core
=====================================================

TreeMap's native scan core is the module treemap_core.node and, on
Windows, the helper tm-mft-helper.exe. Both are built from the Rust
workspace native/treemap-core in TreeMap's repository, and both contain
code written by others: the crates below and the Rust standard library.
Each crate is listed with the licence texts its own package carries.

Every crate in the workspace's Cargo.lock is here, with its licence
texts, whether or not its code ends up in the binaries: "In the
binaries: no" marks one that only runs while they are built, and "yes"
means in the binaries of at least one platform, since a crate for one
operating system's interfaces is linked on that one only.

TreeMap's own crates: tm-governor, tm-mft, tm-mft-helper, tm-node, tm-store, tm-walk.
They are TreeMap's, under its MIT licence (LICENSE in the repository).

gdu, which TreeMap also bundles, carries its own licence: the app keeps
it in its resources as gdu/LICENSE.md, beside this file.

This file is written by scripts/rust-notices.js from Cargo.lock, cargo's
metadata and the Rust toolchain, and sealed by its last line. Do not
edit it by hand: tests/rustNotices.test.ts fails when it no longer
matches Cargo.lock, the generator or its seal.

In the binaries (24):
  bitflags 2.13.2, cfg-if 1.0.5, ctor 0.6.3, dtor 0.1.1, itoa 1.0.18,
  libc 0.2.189, libloading 0.8.9, memchr 2.8.3, napi 3.4.0,
  napi-sys 3.0.1, nohash-hasher 0.2.0, objc2 0.6.4, objc2-encode 4.1.0,
  objc2-foundation 0.3.2, pin-project-lite 0.2.17, rustc-hash 2.1.3,
  serde 1.0.229, serde_core 1.0.229, serde_json 1.0.151,
  thiserror 2.0.20, tokio 1.53.1, windows-link 0.2.1,
  windows-sys 0.61.2, zmij 1.0.23

Used only to build them (15):
  convert_case 0.8.0, ctor-proc-macro 0.0.7, dtor-proc-macro 0.0.6,
  napi-build 2.2.4, napi-derive 3.3.0, napi-derive-backend 3.0.0,
  proc-macro2 1.0.107, quote 1.0.47, semver 1.0.28,
  serde_derive 1.0.229, syn 2.0.119, syn 3.0.6, thiserror-impl 2.0.20,
  unicode-ident 1.0.26, unicode-segmentation 1.13.3

The Rust standard library (1.98.1) and the 11 crates.io crates it is built from follow the crates.
